spec: specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md — replaces the recorded-flaw line\nimport {createChatOrchestrator} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {OrchestratorHooks} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {AIProvider, MessageChunk} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\nimport {PromptBuilder} from '../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\n\nconst SENT_AT = '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z';\n\nfunction createSession(content: string): ChatSession {\n  return {\n    sessionId: 's1',\n    userId: 'u1',\n    entries: [{role: 'user', content, timestamp: SENT_AT}],\n  };\n}\n\n// Returns the rejection reason as a value regardless of its type.\nconst rejectionOf = async (call: Promise<unknown>): Promise<unknown> => {\n  try {\n    await call;\n    return 'resolved without rejecting';\n  } catch (reason: unknown) {\n    return reason;\n  }\n};\n\nfunction throwingProvider(value: unknown): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line require-yield\n    async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      throw value;\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nconst promptBuilder = new PromptBuilder({identity: 'You are a test assistant.'});\n\ndescribe('onError for non-Error rejections (HALEngine#95)', () => {\n  it('calls onError with a wrapped Error when the provider rejects with a string, and re-throws the original string', async () => {\n    let onErrorCallCount = 0;\n    let onErrorArg: Error | undefined;\n    const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n      onError: async (_session, error) => {\n        onErrorCallCount++;\n        onErrorArg = error;\n      },\n    };\n\n    const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(throwingProvider('provider down'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n    const callerRejection = await rejectionOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n    expect(onErrorCallCount).toBe(1);\n    expect(onErrorArg).toBeInstanceOf(Error);\n    expect((onErrorArg as Error & {cause: unknown}).cause).toBe('provider down');\n    expect(callerRejection).toBe('provider down');\n  });\n\n  it('calls onError with a wrapped Error when the provider rejects with a plain object, and re-throws the original object', async () => {\n    const rawRejection = {code: 'RATE_LIMIT', retryAfter: 60};\n    let onErrorCallCount = 0;\n    let onErrorArg: Error | undefined;\n    const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n      onError: async (_session, error) => {\n        onErrorCallCount++;\n        onErrorArg = error;\n      },\n    };\n\n    const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(throwingProvider(rawRejection), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n    const callerRejection = await rejectionOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n    expect(onErrorCallCount).toBe(1);\n    expect(onErrorArg).toBeInstanceOf(Error);\n    expect((onErrorArg as Error & {cause: unknown}).cause).toBe(rawRejection);\n    expect(callerRejection).toBe(rawRejection);\n  });\n\n  it('calls onError with a wrapped Error when the provider rejects with undefined, and re-throws undefined', async () => {\n    let onErrorCallCount = 0;\n    let onErrorArg: Error | undefined;\n    const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n      onError: async (_session, error) => {\n        onErrorCallCount++;\n        onErrorArg = error;\n      },\n    };\n\n    const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(throwingProvider(undefined), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n    const callerRejection = await rejectionOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n    expect(onErrorCallCount).toBe(1);\n    expect(onErrorArg).toBeInstanceOf(Error);\n    expect(callerRejection).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n});\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8558,"cache_read_input_tokens":51565,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":8558},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f596f5ba-7ee8-4678-ac0b-7f4779056738","uuid":"f5c2e885-f6b6-4cbd-a347-c19e2d1ca079","timestamp":"2026-11-06T23:08:52.675Z","request_id":"req_011CgnWtfMeEDzBBo1rMmJPV"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-6","id":"msg_011CgnWvpZMGac9RhESw9sx4","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01F85151bAzjTq6RNbaL7251","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/5cc13ebca147/workspace/target/.lore/dod.md","content":"# Definition of Done\n\n> The orchestrator's `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error`. A rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string, a plain object, a value from a library that rejects with its own shape — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.\n\n**Strategy: `direct`** — The seam already exists: `createChatOrchestrator` accepts an `onError` hook through `ChatOrchestratorOptions.hooks`, and the catch block at `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts:L150` is the exact gate that needs to change.\n\n## Done when these pass\n\n- [ ] **calls onError with a wrapped Error when the provider rejects with a string, and re-throws the original string** — a provider that throws a string calls `onError` once with an `Error` whose `cause` is that string; the caller's rejection is the original string unchanged\n  `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts`\n\n- [ ] **calls onError with a wrapped Error when the provider rejects with a plain object, and re-throws the original object** — same contract for a plain object rejection\n  `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts`\n\n- [ ] **calls onError with a wrapped Error when the provider rejects with undefined, and re-throws undefined** — same contract for `undefined`\n  `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts`\n\n## Facets\n\n- [ ] Change the `instanceof Error` gate in the catch block (`chatOrchestrator.ts:L150`) to call `onError` for every rejection, wrapping non-Error values in `new Error` with the original as `cause`\n- [ ] Re-throw the original value unchanged (not the wrapper) so callers see no behaviour change\n- [ ] Replace the recorded-flaw line in `specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md` with the new guarantee and add VALIDATED_BY links pointing to the acceptance tests\n- [ ] Add a CHANGELOG entry describing the change for existing consumers\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- `beforeSession` escaping the outer `try` (its own issue)\n- Usage accounting across tool rounds (HALEngine#93)\n- Any change to the shape of the re-thrown value at the caller boundary\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":420,"cache_read_input_tokens":63159,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":420},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f596f5ba-7ee8-4678-ac0b-7f4779056738","uuid":"ae51ffdc-7c71-4c65-93f9-71991216e77d","timestamp":"2026-11-06T23:09:17.875Z","request_id":"req_011CgnWvp35woEPo4fFzqvQf"}
